# Near misses
Retained C reconstructions and unresolved screens: starting points, never linked
or counted. Move a file into `src/` once `tools/build.py` places it.
An explicitly recorded compiler or assembler failure is a blocker, not a match.
Recorded candidate sizes, profiles and differences describe the retained experiments, not
verified behavior. Declaration repairs and symbol renaming are not new match measurements.
Drafts marked G8 need a `-g8.c` source unit when checked again.
For entries corrected by the catalogue generator, the size column gives the current
body size. Older draft headers and measurements describe the previous extents; they
must be requalified before integration. Rows marked as removed catalogue remnants
retain historical notes, not functions to promote. The entry repairs do not qualify C.
Extracted drafts may still need the original source unit's types and declarations. Their
recorded checks describe the original compilation unit, not standalone syntax or verified behavior.
Some older game drafts record experiments with the compiler recipe at
`ace3f7be822bae9e85af0ae9010bd5c0dca91986`. Those residuals are historical observations;
later dated entries record their own profiles and measurements. Neither is proof
of semantic correctness or matching credit without a strict linked build.

## Functional C

`functional/` and `functional.tsv` hold C that does what a still-assembly
function does without matching its bytes, written for the native port (see
`functional/README.md` and OpenRAC's `docs/port/BACKFILL.md`). It is never
linked, never counted by `tools/progress.py` (which reads `src/` only), and
`tests/check_repository.py` checks that each listed function is still
`INCLUDE_ASM` in `src/`.

A functional file is done only when the gate says SAME: `tools/fcheck.py`
compiles it in place of the function's `INCLUDE_ASM` line (with the unit's
flags; on its own when that does not compile), places it at 0x000A0000 and
runs OpenRAC's test-only interpreter on call states captured from the US
disc, comparing memory, the result, the kept registers and device accesses
with retail. It covers boot functions called in the boot, title, menu pages
and New Game up to the first level's load, and level 00 (Aranos, the
tutorial): New Game into it, played a while (`level00`). A function with a
place in level 00's program (a `func_L00_` function, or a boot or shared one
with a `00:` place in `config/levels/functions.tsv`) is checked at that
place, its symbols resolved at level 00's addresses; a boot function below
0x1A7C80 stays resident in the level and is checked as the boot's. Other
levels are not covered yet. With OpenRAC's interpreter built
(`build/interpreter/`) and the compiler reachable through `RAC2_LINUX` (on a
Mac, `docker run -d --name rac2-gate --platform linux/386 -v $HOME:$HOME
rac2-build:latest sleep infinity`, then `RAC2_LINUX="docker exec
rac2-gate"`):

```sh
python3 tools/build.py boot                     # build/boot/placed.json: the values placement uses
python3 tools/build.py level 00                 # build/00_aranos_tutorial/placed.json, for level 00
python3 tools/fcheck.py capture LIST            # states of the listed functions (every run)
python3 tools/fcheck.py capture LIST --script level00   # level 00 only (states about 16 MB each)
python3 tools/fcheck.py check func_XXXXXXXX nonmatching/functional/func_XXXXXXXX.c
python3 tools/fcheck.py check func_L00_XXXXXXXX nonmatching/functional/func_L00_XXXXXXXX.c
```

The batch form, which writes the verdicts to OpenRAC's
`port/game/rac2-ntsc/fcheck.tsv`, is OpenRAC's `python3 tools/fcheck_gate.py
run --game rac2` (its `docs/port/BACKFILL.md`, "The gate"). Exit codes and
the placement rules are in `tools/fcheck.py`'s docstring; its tests are
`tests/test_fcheck.py`.

This cc1 always folds a constant offset into the `lq`/`sq`; the retail materialises the pointer first. The fold is in the CSE pass (`find_best_addr` in gcc/cse.c) with `mips_address_cost` giving register+small-constant the same cost as a plain register, so the tie goes to the plus. |

| [`func_L20_0039BDF0`](levels/func_L20_0039BDF0.c) | 212 | 208 bytes; the shape, the frame, the calls, the reload of `arg0` out of `p+0xB8` and the two argument stores all match. The two 16-byte copies differ: the retail materialises the pointer (`addiu $a0,$s0,0x10` / `sq $v0,0x0($a0)`), this compiler folds the constant offset into the `sq`. The fold happens in the CSE pass (`find_best_addr` in gcc/cse.c): the address is `(mem:TI (plus:SI (reg) (const_int off)) 0)` and `mips_address_cost` gives register+small-constant the cost 1, the same as a plain register, so the tie goes to the plus. The TImode displacement range is [-32768, 32752]; an offset outside it makes the reload materialise the address - the retail's shape with the wrong immediate. The float constants are hoisted in front of the copy, and the second call's delay slot holds the store to `arg0+0x98` where the retail has `addiu $a1,$s0,0xF0`. |

What differs: retail keeps the hero block in `$s1` and the resident block in `$s2` (here `$s0`/`$s1`, and the 128-bit temporary follows), retail materialises the address of D_00189EA0+0x250 in a register and reads the quadword from it (`lq $v0,0($s1)`) while this C folds the 0x250 into the `lq`, and retail re-materialises the address of D_00189EA0 from the high part it kept in `$s5`. The two symbols D_00189EA0 and D_00189E20 differ by 0x80 in retail, which the compiler only knows when both are in one unit, so the resident block is written as D_00189EA0-0x80 here |

Wave 8 re-measured it with ten more spellings (int/char/short/void/unsigned base, `index*16` against `index<<4`, the constant first, the address in a local, the 16-byte record array): the order of the two operands follows the *kind* of the base, not the source - a runtime base (a parameter, or the block pointer read through a pointer variable) gives `addu $2,$4,$2` (base first, exactly retail's shape) while a constant base (the symbol's address) goes through the address pattern and gives `addu $2,$2,$4` (index first); the game cc1 and the SDK's ee cc1 behave the same. So the retail's compiler kept the materialised constant in a register for the add and this cc1 folds the address into the address pattern; the source cannot ask for the difference. The body needs a -G8 unit (the retail reaches the first registry word with `lw $a0,-0x1850($gp)`), so it cannot live in the plain unit. |
